Scarcity is therefore produced by control, not merely suffered as lack.","ayah_refs":["70:21","70:27","70:40"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Prayer as Stable Station and Recurrent Guard","parent_semantic_invariant":"Repeated embodied observance turns unstable response into duration, orientation, and vigilant maintenance.","parent_surprising_reach":"Prayer forms a station with direction, posture, orbit, watch, straight path, and a speaking act of blessing.","key":"C","name":"Invocation answered by affirmation","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Blessing or petition is uttered, affirmed, and borne as truthful testimony.","active_motifs":"prayer as invocation, praise, and mercy `quranic:root_000879:B002/m01`; “amen” as request for response `quranic:root_000054:B003/m01`; oath as divided commitment `quranic:root_001226:B004/m01`; informed testimony `quranic:root_000822:B002/m01`; truthful utterance `quranic:root_000852:B001/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:22-23,34` الْمُصَلِّين/صَلَات (`ص ل و`); `70:28,32` مَأْمُون/أَمَانَات (`ء م ن`); `70:26` يُصَدِّقُون (`ص د ق`); `70:33` شَهَادَات (`ش ه د`); `70:40` أُقْسِم (`ق س م`).","synthesis":"The ritual station also has a discourse structure: invocation seeks an answer, affirmation ratifies it, oath binds it, and testimony carries it publicly. Constancy thus joins bodily recurrence to dependable speech.","ayah_refs":["70:22","70:28","70:26","70:33","70:40"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] The Known Right as a Distributive Measure","parent_semantic_invariant":"Wealth becomes legitimate when a visible, fixed share leaves private possession and reaches a claimant.","parent_surprising_reach":"The right behaves as title, joint, measured share, public mark, outgoing revenue, and hospitality at a crowded door.","key":"A","name":"Fixed title, mark, and share","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A claim is identified, marked, measured, and assigned from a privately held stock.","active_motifs":"accumulation of property `quranic:root_001457:B001/m01`; binding entitlement `quranic:root_000347:B002/m01`; privately held right `quranic:root_000347:B003/m01`; establishing a claim `quranic:root_000347:B005/m01`; fitting joint or container `quranic:root_000347:B011/m01`; known information `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`; visible identifying mark `quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`; apportioned share `quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`; allotted portion `quranic:root_001507:B005/m01`; fixed threshold and basis `quranic:root_001507:B006/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:24` أَمْوَالِهِم، حَق، مَعْلُوم (`م و ل`, `ح ق ق`, `ع ل م`); `70:40` أُقْسِم (`ق س م`); `70:43` نُصُب (`ن ص ب`).","synthesis":"The share is “known” because it has legal and perceptual form: title establishes it, measurement bounds it, a mark identifies it, and a fitting joint locates it within the whole estate. Distribution is the correct placement of a part, not discretionary generosity.","ayah_refs":["70:24","70:40","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] The Known Right as a Distributive Measure","parent_semantic_invariant":"Wealth becomes legitimate when a visible, fixed share leaves private possession and reaches a claimant.","parent_surprising_reach":"The right behaves as title, joint, measured share, public mark, outgoing revenue, and hospitality at a crowded door.","key":"C","name":"Manufactured scarcity","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A holder narrows provision, reduces the gift, and invokes prohibition or difference to keep the claimant outside.","active_motifs":"refusal to give `quranic:root_001448:B001/m01`; meager or inferior gift `quranic:root_000803:B001/m01`; miserly reduction `quranic:root_000803:B004/m01`; narrowed provision `quranic:root_001205:B004/m01`; prohibition `quranic:root_000313:B001/m01`; deprivation `quranic:root_000313:B008/m01`; exception and exclusion `quranic:root_001119:B005/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:21` مَنُوعًا (`م ن ع`); `70:24-25` حَق/الْمَحْرُوم (`ح ق ق`, `ح ر م`); `70:27` مُشْفِقُون (`ش ف ق`); `70:28,30` غَيْر (`غ ي ر`); `70:40` قَادِرُون (`ق د ر`).","synthesis":"Scarcity is assembled through deliberate operations: narrow, diminish, prohibit, exclude, and withhold. These motifs pressure the description of human refusal by showing how abundance can be made inaccessible without ceasing to exist.","ayah_refs":["70:21","70:24","70:27","70:28","70:40"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Fear Before Judgment and Humiliation After It","parent_semantic_invariant":"Careful fear anticipates an unsecured danger; when the danger arrives, it surrounds, lowers, and visibly masters the body.","parent_surprising_reach":"Twilight, exposed frontier, pursuing pressure, sinking star, lost hump, low hem, and struck peg all specify the transition from vigilance to abasement.","key":"C","name":"Eye, horizon, and sinking light","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A fixed forward gaze gives way to streaming eyes, sinking celestial bodies, and lowered sight.","active_motifs":"ocular sight `quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`; inward discernment `quranic:root_000121:B002/m01`; prolonged forward gaze `quranic:root_001594:B001/m01`; projected head and neck `quranic:root_001594:B002/m01`; tear channels at the eye's edges `quranic:root_001077:B004/m01`; sharp wet edges of teeth `quranic:root_001077:B005/m01`; star sinking toward setting `quranic:root_000412:B003/m01`; twilight glow `quranic:root_000803:B002/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:44` أَبْصَار (`ب ص ر`); `70:27` مُشْفِقُون (`ش ف ق`); `70:36` مُهْطِعِين (`ه ط ع`); `70:40` مَغَارِب (`غ ر ب`); `70:44` خَاشِعَة (`خ ش ع`).","synthesis":"The crowd first projects eye, head, and neck forward; at the endpoint, sight sinks. Tear channels and setting light translate the reversal into a horizon inside the face, where the gaze reaches its own west.","ayah_refs":["70:44","70:27","70:36","70:40"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Cultivated Enclosure and Honored Residence","parent_semantic_invariant":"The garden is a protected ecology whose shade, water, growth, and settled enjoyment are produced by sustained care.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garden becomes canopy, vineyard, pasture, rain-cloud, well, bucket, camel trough, catchment, fertile soil, and hospitable station.","key":"B","name":"Rain, well, bucket, and trough","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Water descends, collects in rock and well, is drawn by bucket, poured into a trough, and approached by animals.","active_motifs":"rain-holding rock hollow or new well `quranic:root_000434:B011/m01`; layered rain-cloud `quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`; abundant fresh water `quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`; deep abundant well `quranic:root_001040:B005/m01`; great water bucket `quranic:root_001077:B002/m01`; poured well-water and overflowing trough `quranic:root_001077:B003/m01`; watering camels at their mouths `quranic:root_001198:B014/m01`; returning camels for another drink `quranic:root_000464:B007/m01`; water-holding place `quranic:root_001667:B005/m01`; renewing rain `quranic:root_001055:B007/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:19,39` خُلِق/خَلَق (`خ ل ق`); `70:24,39` مَعْلُوم/يَعْلَمُون (`ع ل م`); `70:27-28,40` رَب (`ر ب ب`); `70:32` عَهْد (`ع ه د`); `70:36` قِبَل (`ق ب ل`); `70:38` يُدْخَل (`د خ ل`); `70:40` مَغَارِب (`غ ر ب`); `70:43` يُوفِضُون (`و ف ض`).","synthesis":"The water system is a causal chain from cloud to hollow, well, bucket, trough, and drinking herd. A garden of delight is therefore infrastructural as well as scenic: its ease depends on recurrent delivery and managed access.","ayah_refs":["70:19","70:24","70:27","70:32","70:36","70:38","70:40","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Horizon Oath and Sovereign Replacement","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule over recurring horizons establishes the capacity to redesign and replace a population before it can escape.","parent_surprising_reach":"Cosmic horizon, legal division, measured design, proxy, changed form, and footrace are one executable proof.","key":"A","name":"Recurrent eastings and westings","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"Light rises and sets across multiple horizon points, making daily recurrence a visible domain of rule.","active_motifs":"rising light and eastern horizon `quranic:root_000790:B001/m01`; sunlit place of prayer `quranic:root_000790:B006/m01`; setting sun and western horizon `quranic:root_001077:B006/m01`; exile into distance `quranic:root_001077:B007/m01`; rare or unfamiliar object `quranic:root_001077:B008/m01`; unguided projectile from an unknown source `quranic:root_001077:B013/m01`; far-flying portent `quranic:root_001077:B014/m01`; bounded day `quranic:root_001700:B001/m01`; temporal duration `quranic:root_001700:B002/m01`; twilight `quranic:root_000803:B002/m01`; sinking celestial body `quranic:root_000412:B003/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:27` مُشْفِقُون (`ش ف ق`); `70:40` الْمَشَارِق وَالْمَغَارِب (`ش ر ق`, `غ ر ب`); `70:42-44` يَوْم (`ي و م`); `70:44` خَاشِعَة (`خ ش ع`).","synthesis":"Plural horizons make recurrence spatially visible: each day rises and sets at a shifted point. Exile, unknown-source arrow, and distant portent extend the horizon into domains from which events appear unexpectedly, yet all remain inside the same rule.","ayah_refs":["70:27","70:40","70:42","70:44"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Horizon Oath and Sovereign Replacement","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule over recurring horizons establishes the capacity to redesign and replace a population before it can escape.","parent_surprising_reach":"Cosmic horizon, legal division, measured design, proxy, changed form, and footrace are one executable proof.","key":"B","name":"Oath as division and binding allocation","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"An oath divides responsibility among speakers while fixing portions, terms, and peace between parties.","active_motifs":"apportioning shares `quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`; binding oath `quranic:root_001226:B004/m01`; deliberation divided among alternatives `quranic:root_001226:B006/m01`; negotiated truce `quranic:root_001226:B008/m01`; sworn right hand `quranic:root_001698:B003/m01`; right as strength and entitlement `quranic:root_001698:B004/m01`; oath or adjuration `quranic:root_000076:B007/m01`; limiting particle and quantification `quranic:root_000532:B015/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:27-28,40` رَب (`ر ب ب`); `70:30,37` أَيْمَان/يَمِين (`ي م ن`); `70:40` أُقْسِم (`ق س م`); surface anchor unavailable for `ء ل ي` at `70:43`.","synthesis":"The oath is an allocation of speech and liability: shares are divided, right hands bind, alternatives are weighed, and terms can suspend conflict. It gives formal force to the claim of capacity that follows.","ayah_refs":["70:27","70:30","70:40","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Horizon Oath and Sovereign Replacement","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule over recurring horizons establishes the capacity to redesign and replace a population before it can escape.","parent_surprising_reach":"Cosmic horizon, legal division, measured design, proxy, changed form, and footrace are one executable proof.","key":"C","name":"Design, substitution, and improvement","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A formed population is remeasured, one bearer is put in another's place, and the replacement is judged better.","active_motifs":"prior measuring of a form `quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`; creative production `quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`; completed shape `quranic:root_000434:B003/m01`; effective capacity `quranic:root_001205:B003/m01`; deliberate measurement `quranic:root_001205:B005/m01`; one thing standing in another's place `quranic:root_000095:B001/m01`; changing form without changing material `quranic:root_000095:B002/m01`; alteration and exchange `quranic:root_001119:B003/m01`; selected excellence `quranic:root_000452:B002/m01`; proxy standing in another's place `quranic:root_001273:B007/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:19,39` خُلِق/خَلَقْنَاهُم (`خ ل ق`); `70:21,41` الْخَيْر/خَيْرًا (`خ ي ر`); `70:28,30` غَيْر (`غ ي ر`); `70:33` قَائِمُون (`ق و م`); `70:40` قَادِرُون (`ق د ر`); `70:41` نُبَدِّل (`ب د ل`).","synthesis":"Replacement is specified as a design operation: measure, form, assess, substitute, alter, and improve. Because the same agent both creates and evaluates the form, substitution is not constrained to exchange among preexisting equals.","ayah_refs":["70:19","70:21","70:28","70:33","70:40","70:41"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Horizon Oath and Sovereign Replacement","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule over recurring horizons establishes the capacity to redesign and replace a population before it can escape.","parent_surprising_reach":"Cosmic horizon, legal division, measured design, proxy, changed form, and footrace are one executable proof.","key":"D","name":"The pursued cannot get ahead","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A runner accelerates and tries to create an unbridgeable lead, but pursuit remains able to reach him.","active_motifs":"forward precedence and racing `quranic:root_000671:B001/m01`; escaping beyond a pursuer's reach `quranic:root_000671:B004/m01`; rapid approach `quranic:root_001594:B003/m01`; speed in movement and action `quranic:root_000698:B001/m01`; front rank of a moving host `quranic:root_000698:B002/m01`; urgent rushing `quranic:root_001667:B001/m01`; capacity to execute `quranic:root_001205:B003/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:36` مُهْطِعِين (`ه ط ع`); `70:40` قَادِرُون (`ق د ر`); `70:41` مَسْبُوقِين (`س ب ق`); `70:43` سِرَاعًا، يُوفِضُون (`س ر ع`, `و ف ض`).","synthesis":"“Not outstripped” becomes a chase relation. Speed, front rank, urgent rush, and attempted escape measure the creature's motion; sovereign capacity measures the pursuer's continued ability to close it.","ayah_refs":["70:36","70:40","70:41","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Wading Play Dissolved into Encounter","parent_semantic_invariant":"Aimless activity immerses its participants in a mixed medium until the promised event interrupts and gathers the scattered motion.","parent_surprising_reach":"Futile discourse becomes water-crossing, stirred drink, saliva, venom, wind-erased ground, discarded object, and joined endpoints.","key":"B","name":"Sweetness, saliva, and venom","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Fluids issuing from mouths or animals can nourish, intoxicate, or poison, making surface liquidity morally ambiguous.","active_motifs":"flowing saliva `quranic:root_001358:B002/m01`; honey or venom as animal secretion `quranic:root_001358:B003/m01`; honey retained in comb `quranic:root_000822:B007/m01`; wine covering the mind `quranic:root_000819:B009/m01`; fermented drink `quranic:root_001077:B016/m01`; thick syrup used to preserve or season `quranic:root_000532:B006/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:27-28,40` رَب (`ر ب ب`); `70:33` شَهَادَات (`ش ه د`); `70:37` الشِّمَال (`ش م ل`); `70:40` مَغَارِب (`غ ر ب`); `70:42` يَلْعَبُوا (`ل ع ب`).","synthesis":"The same class of issuing fluid ranges from nourishing honey to intoxicant and venom. This instability clarifies why immersion cannot be judged by pleasant surface flow; its source and effect determine whether it feeds, veils, or kills.","ayah_refs":["70:27","70:33","70:37","70:40","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Wading Play Dissolved into Encounter","parent_semantic_invariant":"Aimless activity immerses its participants in a mixed medium until the promised event interrupts and gathers the scattered motion.","parent_surprising_reach":"Futile discourse becomes water-crossing, stirred drink, saliva, venom, wind-erased ground, discarded object, and joined endpoints.","key":"D","name":"Wind erases the playground","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Wind moves through an exposed place until prior tracks and structures are effaced.","active_motifs":"wind wearing away a dwelling `quranic:root_001358:B005/m01`; broad darkness or water that covers `quranic:root_001307:B002/m01`; north wind and its cooling action `quranic:root_000819:B007/m01`; soft south wind `quranic:root_001525:B009/m01`; western direction and setting `quranic:root_001077:B006/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:36` كَفَرُوا (`ك ف ر`); `70:37` الشِّمَال (`ش م ل`); `70:38` نَعِيم (`ن ع م`); `70:40` مَغَارِب (`غ ر ب`); `70:42` يَلْعَبُوا (`ل ع ب`).","synthesis":"Play's “place” is not durable. Opposed winds traverse it, darkness covers it, and setting removes the light by which its traces were visible. The scene gives the command to leave them a temporal weathering process.","ayah_refs":["70:36","70:37","70:38","70:40","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Race, Wager, and the False Prize","parent_semantic_invariant":"Competitive precedence promises a prize to the swift, but sovereign pursuit removes the contestants' control over both course and award.","parent_surprising_reach":"The scene includes first and second horses, stake, military pay, gambling arrows, divided lots, reward-seeking gift, and losing throw.","key":"B","name":"Gambling kit and apportioned stakes","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Participants put up value, draw or store lots, seek reward, and risk deprivation by the result.","active_motifs":"leather case holding gambling arrows `quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`; winning at gambling by depriving another `quranic:root_000313:B007/m01`; contest in generosity `quranic:root_001294:B006/m01`; gift tendered to obtain recompense `quranic:root_001294:B008/m01`; quiver `quranic:root_001667:B003/m01`; division of lots and shares `quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`; assigned portion `quranic:root_001507:B005/m01`; soldiers' pay as an object of appetite `quranic:root_000951:B003/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:25` الْمَحْرُوم (`ح ر م`); `70:27-28,40` رَب (`ر ب ب`); `70:35` مُكْرَمُون (`ك ر م`); `70:38` يَطْمَع (`ط م ع`); `70:40` أُقْسِم (`ق س م`); `70:43` نُصُب، يُوفِضُون (`ن ص ب`, `و ف ض`).","synthesis":"The coveted garden is lexically pressured by an economy of stakes: lots are housed, portions divided, appetite fixed on pay, and gifts seek return. Honor differs from this wager because it is received in a rightful station rather than won by manipulating the draw.","ayah_refs":["70:25","70:27","70:35","70:38","70:40","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Race, Wager, and the False Prize","parent_semantic_invariant":"Competitive precedence promises a prize to the swift, but sovereign pursuit removes the contestants' control over both course and award.","parent_surprising_reach":"The scene includes first and second horses, stake, military pay, gambling arrows, divided lots, reward-seeking gift, and losing throw.","key":"C","name":"Flight that cannot secure the award","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A contestant tries to escape with the lead or prize, but substitution and superior capacity overtake him.","active_motifs":"flight beyond the pursuer `quranic:root_000671:B004/m01`; effective capacity `quranic:root_001205:B003/m01`; substitution `quranic:root_000095:B001/m01`; alteration `quranic:root_001119:B003/m01`; unguided projectile `quranic:root_001077:B013/m01`; desired but unavailable prize `quranic:root_000951:B001/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:28,30` غَيْر (`غ ي ر`); `70:38` يَطْمَع (`ط م ع`); `70:40` مَغَارِب، قَادِرُون (`غ ر ب`, `ق د ر`); `70:41` نُبَدِّل، مَسْبُوقِين (`ب د ل`, `س ب ق`).","synthesis":"Speed can win only inside rules the racer controls. Here the governing power can change the field and replace the contestant, so attempted flight cannot secure either lead or award.","ayah_refs":["70:28","70:38","70:40","70:41"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Textile Cover, Hidden Interior, and Chromatic Exposure","parent_semantic_invariant":"A surface may shelter or disguise an interior, but wear, opening, and color eventually disclose what the cover carried.","parent_surprising_reach":"Night-cover, wrap, mosquito tent, worn cloth, concealed sword, inner corruption, dyed sign, black raven, and whitening hair form a disclosure process.","key":"C","name":"Color as involuntary disclosure","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Saturated, divided, black, and white colors expose state across bead, flesh, hair, and surface.","active_motifs":"black-and-white agate bead `quranic:root_000243:B005/m01`; two-color maturation `quranic:root_000243:B006/m01`; saturated red or green color `quranic:root_000790:B003/m01`; raven blackness `quranic:root_001077:B010/m01`; congenital whiteness of lash or hair `quranic:root_001077:B012/m01`; red dye or resin `quranic:root_001077:B015/m01`; variegated surface `quranic:root_000400:B007/m01`; thin or inferior cloth `quranic:root_000803:B001/m01`; identifying mark `quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:20` الشَّر، جَزُوعًا (`ش ر ر`, `ج ز ع`); `70:24,39` مَعْلُوم/يَعْلَمُون (`ع ل م`); `70:27` مُشْفِقُون (`ش ف ق`); `70:40` الْمَشَارِق وَالْمَغَارِب (`ش ر ق`, `غ ر ب`); `70:43` يَخْرُجُون (`خ ر ج`).","synthesis":"Color records internal process on a surface: maturation divides tone, saturation intensifies it, black and white mark extremes, and dye fixes a visible sign. Exposure need not remove the cover when the cover itself testifies.","ayah_refs":["70:20","70:24","70:27","70:40","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Food, Fire, and the Throat of Processing","parent_semantic_invariant":"Material is transformed by heat, drying, chewing, swallowing, and digestion into nourishment, residue, or injury.","parent_surprising_reach":"Sun-curing, spark, roasting drippings, kindled fire, stirred drink, saliva, choking, esophagus, feast, and intensified grinding form one alimentary mechanism.","key":"A","name":"Mouth, esophagus, and digestible food","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Food is tasted, chewed, lubricated, swallowed through the esophagus, and judged by whether it sits easily.","active_motifs":"pleasant digestible food `quranic:root_001409:B003/m01`; esophagus `quranic:root_001409:B004/m01`; eating and ceremonial feeding `quranic:root_001409:B005/m01`; light digestible food `quranic:root_001410:B003/m01`; eating and feast-giving `quranic:root_001410:B004/m01`; esophageal passage `quranic:root_001410:B005/m01`; softness and refined food `quranic:root_001525:B002/m01`; intensifying an operation such as grinding `quranic:root_001525:B010/m01`; choking obstruction `quranic:root_000790:B005/m01`; flowing saliva `quranic:root_001358:B002/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:38` امْرِئ، نَعِيم (`م ر ء`, `ن ع م`); `70:40` الْمَشَارِق (`ش ر ق`); `70:42` يَلْعَبُوا (`ل ع ب`).","synthesis":"Nourishment requires a continuous passage from mouth to stomach. Softness, saliva, esophagus, and digestibility maintain that route; choking reveals how a small obstruction converts desirable intake into bodily threat.","ayah_refs":["70:38","70:40","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Food, Fire, and the Throat of Processing","parent_semantic_invariant":"Material is transformed by heat, drying, chewing, swallowing, and digestion into nourishment, residue, or injury.","parent_surprising_reach":"Sun-curing, spark, roasting drippings, kindled fire, stirred drink, saliva, choking, esophagus, feast, and intensified grinding form one alimentary mechanism.","key":"B","name":"Drying, kindling, and rendered drippings","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Food is exposed to sun, cut, heated, and stirred until moisture leaves and fat or blood flows.","active_motifs":"sun-curing meat `quranic:root_000790:B002/m01`; laying food in sun to dry `quranic:root_000787:B002/m01`; flying sparks `quranic:root_000787:B003/m01`; fat dripping from roast meat `quranic:root_000787:B005/m01`; enduring fire and heat `quranic:root_000879:B001/m01`; hidden fire struck from flint `quranic:root_001642:B002/m01`; stirring drink or blood `quranic:root_000446:B003/m01`; cutting meat and incising a wound `quranic:root_001638:B002/m01`; calming a boiling pot with water `quranic:root_000501:B002/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:20` الشَّر (`ش ر ر`); `70:22-23,34` صَلَاة (`ص ل و`); `70:23` دَائِمُون (`د و م`); `70:31` وَرَاء (`و ر ي`); `70:40` الْمَشَارِق (`ش ر ق`); `70:42` يَخُوضُوا، ذَرْهُم (`خ و ض`, `و ذ ر`).","synthesis":"Processing alternates removal and addition: sun removes moisture, cutting opens matter, fire renders fat, stirring mixes fluid, and water quiets boil. ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
